Result: 在着丝粒定位任务中，该方法在多种基因组大小的物种上均能准确恢复着丝粒的基因组位置。

Conclusion: 所提出的数据驱动方法能有效解决相互作用图谱中的逆问题，着丝粒定位实验验证了其跨物种泛化能力和准确性。

Abstract: Inference from interaction maps, such as centromere identification from genome-wide chromosome conformation capture techniques -- notably Hi-C -- can be formulated as a generic inverse problem: infer a set of parameters given a map summarizing pairwise interactions between entities through blocks of variable numbers and sizes. In this work, we introduce a data-driven approach that leverages shared structure between these maps, such as global alignment between localized patterns, while handling the variability in number and size of entities arising in real-world data. Our approach relies on a transformer architecture capable of handling such variability and a custom simulator to generate abundant, yet computationally cheap synthetic data for training. Applied to the problem of centromere localization, the method accurately recovers their genomic positions across a wide range of species of various genome sizes.

</details>


### [73] [EntmaxKV: Support-Aware Decoding for Entmax Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21649)
*Gonçalo Duarte,Miguel Couceiro,Marcos V. Treviso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EntmaxKV利用α-entmax的稀疏特性，提出了一种在KV缓存加载前即可利用稀疏性的解码框架，通过查询感知的页面评分、支持感知的候选选择和稀疏entmax注意力，在长上下文场景下实现了高达3.36倍（softmax）和5.43倍（entmax）的加速，同时保持与全缓存entmax接近的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文解码中，KV缓存内存流量成为主要瓶颈，因为每个生成token需要注意力覆盖随上下文长度线性增长的缓存。现有稀疏解码方法适用于softmax注意力，但其稠密尾部导致任何截断都会丢弃非零概率质量。而α-entmax能产生精确零值，可将稀疏解码从稠密尾部近似转化为支持恢复问题，但现有entmax核未解决自回归解码瓶颈——在稀疏性未知前仍需流式传输完整KV缓存。

Method: EntmaxKV包含三个核心组件：1）查询感知的页面评分，在加载前评估页面重要性；2）支持感知的候选选择，确保entmax支持被包含；3）稀疏entmax注意力计算。同时引入高斯感知的entmax选择器，通过轻量级页面统计估计entmax阈值，自适应调整所选预算。理论上通过丢弃概率质量δ分析截断误差，证明当entmax支持被恢复时输出误差消失。

Result: 在长上下文和语言建模基准测试中，EntmaxKV在匹配KV预算下比基于softmax的稀疏解码丢弃更少的概率质量、保留更多的支持token、实现更低的输出误差。在1M上下文长度下，相对于全注意力基线，实现高达3.36倍（softmax）和5.43倍（entmax）的速度提升。

Conclusion: EntmaxKV通过entmax原生的稀疏解码框架有效解决了长上下文KV缓存瓶颈，理论保证了稀疏解码的精确性，实验验证了其在效率和效果上的优势，为长上下文LLM推理提供了一种高效的稀疏注意力方案。

Abstract: Long-context decoding is increasingly limited by KV-cache memory traffic since each generated token attends over a cache whose size grows linearly with context length. Existing sparse decoding methods reduce this cost by selecting subsets of tokens or pages, but are designed for softmax attention, whose dense tails make any truncation discard nonzero probability mass. In contrast, $α$-entmax produces exact zeros, turning sparse decoding from dense-tail approximation into support recovery: if the selected candidates contain the entmax support, sparse decoding remains exact. While recent entmax kernels enable efficient training, they do not address the autoregressive decoding bottleneck, where dense inference still streams the full KV cache before sparsity is known. In this work, we introduce EntmaxKV, an entmax-native sparse decoding framework that exploits sparsity before KV pages are loaded. EntmaxKV combines query-aware page scoring, support-aware candidate selection, and sparse entmax attention. We analyze truncation error through the dropped probability mass $δ$, showing that output error is controlled by $δ$ and vanishes when the entmax support is recovered. We further introduce a Gaussian-aware entmax selector that estimates the entmax threshold from lightweight page statistics, adapting the selected budget to the score distribution. Empirically, EntmaxKV drops less probability mass, retains more support tokens, and achieves lower output error than softmax-based sparse decoding at matched KV budgets. On long-context and language modeling benchmarks, it closely matches full-cache entmax while using a small fraction of the KV cache, achieving up to $3.36\times$ (softmax) and $5.43\times$ (entmax) speedup over full attention baselines at 1M context length. Code available at: https://github.com/deep-spin/entmaxkv.

</details>


### [74] [Amplifying, Not Learning: Fine-Tuned AI Text Detectors Amplify a Pretrained Direction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21653)
*Alexander Smirnov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AI文本检测器本质上是放大了预训练空间中一个固有的"典型性"主轴，而非构建真正的人机边界。该轴在原始编码器上即可取得接近甚至超越微调的性能，但对非母语写作者失效（AUROC低至0.06-0.20），且各种检测方法在该轴上高度一致（observer-invariance），几乎所有检测差异都来自校准偏差而非表征学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示AI文本检测器的本质机制：当前检测器被普遍认为学习了"AI vs 人类"的判别边界，但本文质疑这一解释，提出检测器实际上只是放大了预训练空间中本就存在的"典型性"主轴（typicality axis），而非构建了一个真正的二分类边界。

Method: 在多个架构（RoBERTa-base、ELECTRA等）上，比较原始编码器的质心投影与完整微调的分类性能；利用非母语（ESL）写作文本作为反例验证典型性假说的唯一可证伪预测；设计三种操作上不同的探针（文本表面特征残差化、几何符号消融、闭式文本对预测器）验证observer-invariance；通过匹配TPR=0.90的校准分析量化校准偏差对检测差异的贡献。

Result: 原始编码器的质心投影在NYT-vs-HC3任务上AUROC达到0.806/0.944/0.834（为微调判别上限的86-106%）；对非母语写作AUROC仅0.06-0.20（典型性假说的唯一正确预测）；三种探针在三个架构上一致（cos 0.74/0.81/1.00）；27种干预条件的校准等价（|ΔAUROC| ≤ 0.0081）；LoRA→全微调偏差的≥97%为校准偏移。

Conclusion: AI文本检测器并未学习到真正的人机边界，而是沿用了预训练空间中固有的典型性主轴；这一发现对非母语写作者存在严重的公平性问题，且意味着当前检测器的"改进"主要来自校准优化而非表征学习，需要重新审视检测范式的理论基础。

Abstract: AI text detectors amplify a pretrained typicality axis; they do not construct an AI-vs-human boundary. On raw encoders before any task supervision, projecting onto centroid(AI)-centroid(HC3) achieves NYT-vs-HC3 AUROC 0.806/0.944/0.834 across three architectures (86-106% of the fine-tuned discrimination ceiling: on RoBERTa-base, raw projection exceeds fine-tuning); on RoBERTa-base, full fine-tuning reduces discrimination below raw on both fluent-formal populations tested. The same axis inverts on non-native ESL writing (AUROC 0.06-0.20) -- a falsifiable prediction unique to the typicality reading. A 24-example frozen probe matches full fine-tuning (0.900 vs 0.895). A closed-form Jacobian predictor parameterises axis-manipulating interventions with R^2 = 1.000 universal, lifts ELECTRA-CE deployment TPR from 0.000 to 0.904 at FPR = 1%, and transfers to three independently-trained third-party RoBERTa detectors at 16/16 oracle-equivalence (57% NYT-FPR reduction on the OpenAI detector). Scope: encoder family; mechanism magnitude HC3-anchored; population-level shared axis with per-text mechanisms varying across architectures. Three operationally distinct probes -- text-surface caps_rate residualisation, geometric signed-epsilon ablation, closed-form text-pair predictor -- agree at cos 0.74/0.81/1.00 across three architectures, confirming observer-invariance. Under matched-TPR-0.90 evaluation, the published intervention zoo (CC, dealign-f2c) is calibration-equivalent across 27 cells (|Delta AUROC| <= 0.0081), and >= 97% of the LoRA->full-FT bias gap on ELECTRA is calibration shift, not learned representation -- the central claim's prediction confirmed.

</details>


### [75] [Value-Gradient Hypothesis of RL for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21654)
*Arip Asadulaev,Daniil Ognev,Karim Salta,Martin Takac*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从值梯度视角解释了无评论家强化学习（如PPO、GRPO）在大语言模型后训练中的有效性，提出其在可微展开和加性噪声参数化下，演员更新在期望上等价于值梯度；对于离散transformer策略，自动微分通过注意力机制产生近似值信号的经验伴随状态。据此分解RL影响为值梯度信号和可达奖励空间，给出预训练轨迹中RL最有效的判别准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解无评论家强化学习方法（如PPO、GRPO）在大语言模型后训练中为何有效，以及何时能带来最大收益。

Method: 提出值梯度视角（value-gradient perspective），通过可微展开和加性噪声参数化证明演员更新的期望等于值梯度；分析离散transformer策略中自动微分通过注意力机制产生近似值信号的经验伴随状态，并建立误差控制（采样间隔和策略熵）。

Result: 1. 无评论家RL的演员更新在期望上等价于值梯度；2. 离散transformer中自动微分通过注意力产生近似值信号；3. 提出RL效果分解为值梯度信号和可达奖励头寸，给出RL最有效的判别准则。

Conclusion: 无评论家RL方法在LLM后训练中的有效性可从值梯度视角得到理论解释，并据此预测RL在预训练轨迹中何时最具优势。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning substantially improves pretrained language models, but it remains understudied why critic-free methods such as PPO and GRPO work as well as they do, and when they should provide the largest gains. We develop a value-gradient perspective of critic-free RL for LLM post-training. First, under a differentiable rollout and additive-noise parameterization, we show that the actor update is value-gradient-like in expectation: the backward pass propagates costates whose conditional expectation equals the value gradient. Second, for discrete transformer policies, we show that autodifferentiation through attention produces empirical costates that approximate this value signal, with an error controlled by the sampling gap and policy entropy. These results motivate a decomposition of RL impact into value gradient signal and reachable reward headroom, yielding a criterion for when RL should be most effective along a pretraining trajectory.

</details>


### [76] [Hierarchical Variational Policies for Reward-Guided Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21661)
*Kushagra Pandey,Farrin Marouf Sofian,Jan Niklas Groeneveld,Felix Draxler,Stephan Mandt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于分层变分模型的框架，将测试时适应策略摊销为轻量级随机策略，实现少步扩散采样，在显著降低推理成本的同时生成高质量奖励对齐样本。在4倍超分任务上，该方法相比最优基线实现5倍以上加速且感知质量更优；半摊销版本结合廉价摊销提议与有限测试时优化，在多个逆问题上达到最先进感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练扩散模型在下游任务（如逆问题）的适应通常需要昂贵的测试时引导或优化，现有方法推理成本高，亟需在保证样本质量的前提下大幅降低推理开销。

Method: 将测试时适应形式化为分层变分模型，将控制量摊销为轻量级随机策略，支持大步长少步扩散采样；进一步扩展到半摊销模式，结合廉价摊销提议与有限测试时优化。

Result: 在4倍超分任务上，感知质量优于基线且推理速度提升5倍以上；半摊销模式在多个挑战性逆问题上达到最先进感知质量。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了质量与速度的强平衡，完全摊销版本匹配或超越近期测试时扩展基线，半摊销版本实现感知质量SOTA，验证了分层变分建模在扩散模型高效适应中的有效性。

Abstract: Adapting pretrained diffusion models to downstream objectives such as inverse problems often requires expensive test-time guidance or optimization. We propose a principled framework for generating high-quality reward-aligned samples at substantially reduced inference cost. Our approach formulates test-time adaptation as a hierarchical variational model, where control is amortized into a lightweight yet expressive stochastic policy. This formulation naturally supports few-step diffusion sampling: large step sizes enable fast inference, while the learned policy maintains sample quality by providing structured per-step control. The resulting fully amortized sampler achieves a strong quality--speed tradeoff, matching or exceeding recent test-time scaling baselines while requiring significantly less compute. For example, on 4x super-resolution, our method achieves better perceptual quality with more than 5x faster inference compared to the best-performing baseline. We further extend our approach to a semi-amortized regime that combines cheap amortized proposals with limited test-time optimization, achieving state-of-the-art perceptual quality across several challenging inverse problems.

</details>


### [77] [I-SAFE: Wasserstein Coherence Metrics for Structural Auditing of Scientific AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21731)
*Barbara Tarantino,Gennaro Auricchio,Paolo Giudici*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出I-SAFE框架，一种基于Wasserstein相干性度量的后验分布审计方法，用于评估科学AI模型是否真正利用了领域相关的结构特征，而非数据集中的捷径或偏差。在药物-靶标相互作用预测任务中，该框架揭示了具有相似预测性能的模型存在截然不同的分布响应特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在科学预测任务中表现优异，但强基准性能常被误读为模型学到了有科学意义的行为。模型可能利用捷径特征、数据集特定规律或分布偏差来获得高预测性能，这些特征在留出数据上有效但与领域相关结构不一致，传统基于准确率的评估无法识别这一问题。

Method: 提出I-SAFE后验分布审计框架，核心是Wasserstein相干性度量(WCM)。给定训练好的黑箱预测器和外部结构先验知识，该方法在结构引导的输入扰动下评估原始模型输出，通过三个互补指标度量输出分布相干性：基于分位数的度量(QBM)评估位置级相干性、WCM评估序数相干性、平移不变WCM变体评估形状相干性。

Result: 在Davis激酶基准数据集上，使用KLIFS结合口袋注释和三个序列-based DTI模型(DeepConvDTI, DeepDTA, TAPB)进行实验。尽管这些模型具有可比的预测性能，I-SAFE揭示了它们截然不同的分布响应特征，这种差异在基于准确率的评估中不可见。

Conclusion: I-SAFE框架是模型无关的，适用于任何输入可进行结构化分解且存在外部先验知识的领域。它能够揭示模型在分布层面的行为差异，为科学AI模型的可信评估提供了超越传统准确率指标的新工具。

Abstract: Deep learning models are increasingly used in scientific prediction tasks where strong benchmark performance is often interpreted as evidence of scientifically meaningful behavior. This interpretation is fragile, as models may exploit shortcut features, dataset-specific regularities, or distributional biases that are predictive on held-out data but not aligned with domain-relevant structure. To address this limitation, we introduce the \textsc{I-SAFE} (Interventional Secure, Accurate, Fair and Explainable) framework, a post-hoc distributional auditing framework for scientific AI models centered on the Wasserstein Coherence Metric (WCM). Given a trained black-box predictor and an external structural prior encoding domain knowledge about task-relevant input structure, \textsc{I-SAFE} evaluates raw model outputs under structurally guided perturbations of the input. The proposed audit measures output-distribution coherence through three complementary metrics: a Quantile-Based Metric (QBM) for location-level coherence, the WCM for ordinal coherence, and a translation-invariant WCM variant for shape coherence. We instantiate \textsc{I-SAFE} on drug--target interaction (DTI) prediction using the Davis kinase benchmark, KLIFS (Kinase--Ligand Interaction Fingerprints and Structures) binding-pocket annotations, and three sequence-based DTI models: DeepConvDTI, DeepDTA, and TAPB. Although the models operate in a comparable predictive regime, \textsc{I-SAFE} reveals substantially different distributional response profiles, a distinction invisible to accuracy-based evaluation. The framework is model-agnostic and applicable to any domain where inputs admit a structured decomposition and an external prior is available.

</details>


### [78] [Correcting Class Imbalance in Prior-Data Fitted Networks for Tabular Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21742)
*Samuel McDowell,Nathan Stromberg,Lalitha Sankar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了类别不平衡对PFN分类器的影响，并验证了阈值调整和下采样技术在PFN上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PFN在表格分类任务中表现优异，但类别不平衡会导致其在稀有类别上性能下降。现有缓解不平衡的技术在PFN的上下文学习动态下，损失函数策略不可行，其他技术也未经充分验证。

Method: 将多种经典类别不平衡处理技术（如阈值调整、下采样等）适配到PFN分类框架中，并评估其性能。

Result: 阈值调整因PFN的校准特性表现优异；下采样因PFN在有限数据下的卓越性能而表现相当，且能降低推理计算成本。

Conclusion: 阈值调整和下采样是处理PFN类别不平衡问题的有效方法，前者依赖校准特性，后者兼顾性能与效率。

Abstract: Prior-data fitted networks (PFNs) have achieved exceptional performance on tabular classification tasks. However, like other classifiers, their performance can suffer under the effect of class imbalance, resulting in poor performance for rare classes. Several techniques exist which attempt to mitigate the deleterious effect of class imbalance on classification performance, but the in-context learning (ICL) dynamic of PFNs means that loss-based strategies are impossible, and other techniques are unproven. We have adapted several classical techniques addressing class imbalance and analyzed their performance on PFN classification. We observe that thresholding performs exceptionally well because of the calibration characteristics of PFNs, and downsampling performs comparably because of PFNs exceptional limited-data performance, with the additional benefit of reduced computation cost for inference.

</details>


### [79] [Quantitative coronary calcification analysis for prediction of myocardial ischemia using non-contrast CT calcium scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21745)
*Juhwan Lee,Sadeer Al-Kindi,Ammar Hoori,Tao Hu,Hao Wu,Justin N. Kim,Robert Gilkeson,Sanjay Rajagopalan,David L. Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究基于常规非增强CT钙化扫描（CTCS），利用机器学习框架结合钙组学特征预测心肌缺血，在987名患者中达到98.9%的精确度和79.2%的敏感性，钙组学特征显著提升了预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非增强CT钙化评分已广泛用于心血管风险分层，但能否从常规CTCS扫描中预测心肌缺血尚不明确。本研究旨在开发一种机器学习框架，利用定量冠脉钙化评估从常规非增强CTCS扫描中预测心肌缺血，实现更便捷的心血管风险分层。

Method: 回顾性分析1,375名患者，纳入74个变量（临床变量、Agatston评分、钙组学特征）。使用XGBoost结合SHAP筛选特征，采用5折交叉验证训练和评估模型。最终模型包含Agatston评分、8个钙组学特征和年龄。

Result: 在987名患者中，89例（9%）存在心肌缺血。模型精确度98.9±3.0%，敏感性79.2±8.4%，F1分数87.7±5.3%。钙组学特征显著提升预测性能（p<0.05）。钙化动脉数量在SHAP分析中排名最低，但逻辑回归显示其与心肌缺血关联最强（OR: 3.63, 95%CI: 2.80-4.77, p<0.00001）。

Conclusion: 成功开发了基于常规非增强CTCS扫描预测心肌缺血的机器学习方法。钙组学特征在传统风险因素和Agatston评分基础上提供了增量预测价值，有助于实现更易获取的心血管风险分层。

Abstract: Non-contrast computed tomography calcium scoring (CTCS) is widely recognized as an effective tool for cardiovascular risk stratification. This study aimed to develop a novel machine learning framework for predicting myocardial ischemia from routine non-contrast CTCS scans using quantitative coronary calcium assessment. This study analyzed 1,375 patients who underwent both non-contrast CTCS and regadenoson stress cardiac positron emission tomography myocardial perfusion imaging within one year at University Hospitals Cleveland Medical Center. A total of 74 variables, including clinical variables, Agatston score, and calcium-omics features, were evaluated. Relevant features were identified using XGBoost with Shapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP). Predictive models were trained and evaluated using 5-fold cross-validation. Among 987 patients, 89 (9%) were positive for myocardial ischemia. The final model incorporated the Agatston score, eight calcium-omics features, and age. The proposed model achieved a precision of 98.9+/-3.0%, sensitivity of 79.2+/-8.4, and F1 score of 87.7+/-5.3%. The addition of calcium-omics features significantly improved predictive performance compared with models using clinical variables alone or clinical variables with the Agatston score (p<0.05). Interestingly, the number of calcified arteries, despite being the lowest-ranked feature based on SHAP analysis, showed the strongest association with myocardial ischemia in logistic regression analysis (odds ratio: 3.63, 95% confidence interval: 2.80-4.77, p<0.00001). We developed a machine learning approach for predicting myocardial ischemia using routinely acquired non-contrast CTCS scans. Calcium-omics features provided incremental predictive value beyond conventional risk factors and Agatston scoring and may support more accessible cardiovascular risk stratification.

</details>


### [80] [Models Can Model, But Can't Bind: Structured Grounding in Text-to-Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21751)
*Zhiqi Gao,Albert Ge,Alexander Berenbeim,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Text2Opt-Bench基准和BIND方法，发现文本到优化任务中存在有效绑定极限（随着实例数据增长准确率崩溃），BIND通过将数值数据外化到结构化文件实现程序化绑定，显著提升GPT系列模型准确率，且绑定专用微调模型优于端到端方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到优化需要建模与绑定两种能力，现有方法在数据规模增长时准确率急剧下降，缺乏对此问题的系统性研究与解决方案。

Method: 构建Text2Opt-Bench基准（12类优化问题，含LP、随机优化、多目标优化等）；提出BIND推理方法，将数值数据外化到结构化文件，使模型以编程方式而非从提示中转录来实现绑定。

Result: GPT-5-Nano从59.1%提升至82.4%（匹配pass@5的82.0%且token成本更低）；GPT-5从86.2%提升至95.8%；1.5B绑定专用模型可匹敌7B端到端基线。

Conclusion: 绑定（binding）是文本到优化任务的关键瓶颈，通过外化数值数据可有效突破有效绑定极限；绑定是一种可独立学习且泛化能力强的能力。

Abstract: Text-to-optimization requires two separable capabilities: modeling -- choosing the right optimization structure -- and binding -- grounding every coefficient, index, and parameter in the concrete problem data. We study this via Text2Opt-Bench, a scalable benchmark of solver-verified optimization problems spanning 12 categories, from textbook linear programs to stochastic and multi-objective formulations with up to thousands of variables. Across 10+ models, we find that accuracy collapses as instance data grows, even when the formulation itself is simple. We call this the effective binding limit. We address this via a simple inference-time approach, BIND, which externalizes numeric data to structured files so the model binds data programmatically rather than transcribing from the prompt. BIND improves GPT-5-Nano from 59.1% to 82.4% accuracy, matching pass@5 (82.0%) at lower token cost than pass@1, and GPT-5 from 86.2% to 95.8%. Furthermore, we validate our hypothesis by finetuning a model exclusively on binding and show that it outperforms end-to-end SFT and RL across three structurally distinct optimization categories, with a 1.5B binding specialist alone matching a 7B end-to-end baseline.

</details>


### [81] [PEARL: Unbiased Percentile Estimation via Contrastive Learning for Industrial-Scale Livestream Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21752)
*Blake Gella,Wei Wu,Yuhao Yin,Zexi Huang,Zikai Wang,Emily Liu,Junlin Zhang,Wentao Guo,Qinglei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对推荐系统中用户行为强度不平衡问题，提出非参数对比百分位近似框架PEARL，通过建模相对偏好信号替代绝对交互强度，利用真实对比样本直接估计百分位关系，并结合预测引导的bootstrap平滑、广义值加权与协同训练策略，在离线实验和数十亿用户规模的直播平台在线A/B测试中显著提升推荐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统受用户行为强度不平衡困扰——不同用户的参与模式差异导致观测交互信号无法真实反映偏好，高活跃用户信号被过度放大而低活跃用户被低估，损害推荐质量和鲁棒性。

Method: 提出PEARL框架：1）非参数对比百分位近似，利用真实对比交互样本直接估计百分位关系，无需辅助分布估计模型；2）预测引导的bootstrap机制处理稀疏离散反馈的百分位平滑；3）广义值加权与协同训练策略增强灵活性和表示学习。

Result: 离线实验有效缓解行为偏差并提升多排序目标表现；在线A/B测试（数十亿用户直播平台）获得Watch Duration +2.10%、Consumption Amount +0.80%、Interaction Rate +1.49%、Report Rate -6.91%的显著提升。

Conclusion: PEARL通过相对偏好信号建模有效缓解行为强度不平衡问题，理论证明成对比较可无偏估计百分位偏好信号，在实际大规模系统中验证了有效性。

Abstract: Recommender systems trained on user interaction data are susceptible to behavioral intensity imbalance--a systematic distortion arising from heterogeneous engagement patterns across users. This imbalance skews feedback signals such that observed interactions no longer faithfully reflect true preferences, causing models to disproportionately amplify signals from highly active users while underrepresenting others, which ultimately degrades recommendation quality and robustness at scale. To address this issue, we propose a nonparametric contrastive percentile approximation framework, PEARL, that models relative preference signals instead of absolute engagement magnitudes. Building upon relative advantage debiasing, PEARL leverages real contrastive interaction samples to approximate percentile relationships directly, without relying on auxiliary distribution estimation models. We provide theoretical justification demonstrating that such pairwise comparisons yield unbiased estimates of percentile-based preference signals. For broader applicability, we introduce a prediction-based bootstrapping mechanism for percentile smoothing to handle sparse and discrete feedback, alongside a generalized value-weighted formulation and a co-training strategy to enhance both modeling flexibility and representation learning. Extensive offline experiments demonstrate that PEARL effectively mitigates behavioral bias and consistently improves recommendation performance across multiple ranking targets. Deployed in a production livestream platform with a combined user base of billions, online A/B testing confirms substantial real-world gains: +2.10% Watch Duration, +0.80% Consumption Amount, +1.49% Interaction Rate, and -6.91% Report Rate.

</details>


### [82] [Machine learning prediction of obstructive coronary artery disease using opportunistic coronary calcium and epicardial fat assessments from CT calcium scoring scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21762)
*Juhwan Lee,Ammar Hoori,Tao Hu,Justin N. Kim,Mohamed H. E. Makhlouf,Michelle C. Williams,David E. Newby,Robert Gilkeson,Sanjay Rajagopalan,David L. Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究基于SCOT-HEART临床试验中1,324名患者的非增强CT钙化积分图像，提取24项临床变量、189个钙质组学特征和211个心外膜脂肪组学特征，通过CatBoost-SHAP方法筛选出14个最具预测性的特征（前两个来自脂肪组学，其余来自钙质组学），构建了预测阻塞性冠状动脉疾病的机器学习模型，实现了83.1%敏感性、93.8%特异性、85.3%准确率和73.9%F1分数的优异表现，在零钙化积分患者中也展现了可靠预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种利用非增强CT钙化积分图像中冠脉钙化和心外膜脂肪的定量分析来预测阻塞性冠状动脉疾病的先进机器学习框架，以减少对有创或对比剂增强诊断程序的依赖。

Method: 从SCOT-HEART临床试验中1,324名患者的CTCS图像中提取424个候选特征（24项临床变量、189个钙质组学特征、211个心外膜脂肪组学特征），采用CatBoost算法结合SHAP值进行特征选择，最终使用CatBoost梯度提升方法基于14个最有效的特征构建预测模型。

Result: 优化模型实现了83.1±4.6%敏感性、93.8±1.7%特异性、85.3±2.0%准确率和73.9±3.3%F1分数；前两个最重要的预测特征来自脂肪组学；在零钙化积分患者中也保持可靠预测性能。

Conclusion: 该创新方法有望改善临床决策，减少对对比剂增强或侵入性诊断程序的依赖，尤其适用于低至中风险患者群体。

Abstract: Non-contrast computed tomography calcium scoring (CTCS) is a cost-effective imaging modality widely used to detect coronary artery calcifications. This study aimed to develop an advanced machine learning framework that utilizes quantitative analyses of coronary calcium and epicardial fat from CTCS images to predict obstructive coronary artery disease (CAD). The study population consisted of 1,324 patients from the SCOT-HEART clinical trial who underwent both CTCS and coronary CT angiography. We extracted and analyzed a broad range of features, including 24 clinical variables, 189 calcium-omics, and 211 epicardial fat-omics features from the CTCS images. Feature selection was conducted using the CatBoost algorithm combined with SHapley Additive exPlanation (SHAP) values. Predictive modeling utilized the CatBoost gradient boosting method, focusing on the most informative features. From an initial set of 424 candidate features, 14 were identified as most predictive through the CatBoost-SHAP method. The top two predictive features originated from fat-omics, with the remaining 12 features derived from calcium-omics. The optimized model achieved robust predictive capabilities, demonstrating a sensitivity of 83.1+/-4.6%, specificity of 93.8+/-1.7%, accuracy of 85.3+/-2.0%, and an F1 score of 73.9+/-3.3%. Inclusion of calcium-omics and fat-omics data significantly improved predictive performance. Notably, the model also showed reliable predictive accuracy in patients with diverse coronary calcium scores, including cases with obstructive CAD despite a zero-calcium score. This innovative approach holds promise for improving clinical decision-making and potentially reducing dependence on contrast-enhanced or invasive diagnostic procedures, particularly within low-to intermediate-risk patient groups.

</details>


### [83] [On the Sample Complexity of Discounted Reinforcement Learning with Optimized Certainty Equivalents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21763)
*Oliver Mortensen,Mohammad Sadegh Talebi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限折扣MDP中基于生成模型的递推OCE风险度量下的风险敏感强化学习，给出了PAC可学习性的精确刻画，证明了当效用函数定义域非全实数时问题不可PAC学习，并提供了价值学习和策略学习的样本复杂度上下界，其中对于CVaRₜ给出了关于τ的1/τ²的最优依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有风险敏感强化学习研究缺乏对递推OCE风险度量下样本复杂度的系统理解，特别是不同效用函数对PAC可学习性的影响尚不明确。

Method: 采用基于模型的生成学习范式，对递推OCE框架下的风险敏感MDP进行理论分析，通过刻画效用函数的定义域性质与PAC可学习性之间的关系，推导样本复杂度上界，并构造匹配的下界。

Result: (1) 给出了效用函数u使OCE目标PAC可学习的精确条件；(2) 当dom(u)≠ℝ时问题不可PAC学习；(3) 给出了价值学习和策略学习的PAC样本复杂度上界；(4) 建立了状态-动作空间规模S·A意义上的紧下界；(5) 对CVaRₜ给出了关于τ的1/τ²依赖，较现有最优结果提升1/τ因子。

Conclusion: 递推OCE风险度量下的风险敏感强化学习在有限MDP中具有可学习的充分必要条件由效用函数的定义域决定，且对于特定风险度量如CVaRₜ，样本复杂度在τ维度上优于现有结果。

Abstract: We study risk-sensitive reinforcement learning in finite discounted MDPs, where a generative model of the MDP is assumed to be available. We consider a family or risk measures called the optimized certainty equivalent (OCE), which includes important risk measures such as entropic risk, CVaR, and mean-variance. Our focus is on the sample complexities of learning the optimal state-action value function (value learning) and an optimal policy (policy learning) under recursive OCE. We provide an exact characterization of utility functions $u$ for which the corresponding OCE defines an objective that is PAC-learnable. We analyze a simple model-based approach and derive PAC sample complexity bounds. We establish that whenever $u$ does not have full domain $\text{dom}(u)\neq \mathbb{R}$, the corresponding problem is not PAC-learnable. Finally, we establish corresponding lower bounds for both value and policy learning, demonstrating tightness in the size $SA$ of state-action space, and for a more restricted class of utilities, we derive lower bounds that makes the dependence on the effective horizon $\frac{1}{1-γ}$ explicit. Specifically, for $\text{CVaR}_τ$ we show that the correct dependence on $τ$ is $\frac{1}{τ^2}$, thus improving by a factor of $\frac{1}τ$ over state-of-the-art although our bound has a suboptimal dependence on $\frac{1}{1-γ}$.

</details>


### [84] [Memory-R2: Fair Credit Assignment for Long-Horizon Memory-Augmented LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21768)
*Sikuan Yan,Ahmed Bahloul,Ercong Nie,Susanna Schwarzmann,Riccardo Trivisonno,Volker Tresp,Yunpu Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Memory-R2框架，通过LoGo-GRPO算法解决记忆增强型LLM智能体在多会话强化学习中因记忆状态不同导致轨迹对比不公平的问题，采用局部-全局分组优化、共享参数协同设计和渐进式课程学习，实现长期记忆的高效训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GRPO等分组相对方法假设不同轨迹采样自相同环境，但记忆增强型LLM智能体在不同 rollout 中写入、更新或删除不同记忆后，中间记忆状态不再一致，导致轨迹级比较不公平，长程记忆操作的信用分配信号存在噪声或偏差。

Method: 提出Memory-R2框架，核心算法LoGo-GRPO包含：(1) 全局目标保留端到端长程轨迹级奖励学习；(2) 局部重rollout从相同中间记忆状态比较不同记忆操作结果，实现更公平分组比较。采用共享参数协同设计，事实提取器和记忆管理器由同一LLM骨干通过角色提示实例化。采用渐进式课程学习，将训练会话数从8逐步扩展到16再到32。

Result: LoGo-GRPO在长程多会话设置中提供了更精确的记忆构建监督信号，有效解决了记忆状态不同导致的信用分配问题，整体框架在长期记忆增强型LLM智能体训练中表现出色。

Conclusion: Memory-R2为记忆增强型LLM智能体在长程多会话场景下的训练提供了一种有效范式，通过局部-全局分组优化、共享参数协同设计和渐进式课程学习三组件的有机结合，解决了传统分组方法在记忆环境下的根本性缺陷。

Abstract: Memory-augmented LLM agents enable interactions that extend beyond finite context windows by storing, updating, and reusing information across sessions. However, training such agents with reinforcement learning in multi-session environments is challenging because memory turns the agent's past actions into part of its future environment. Once different rollouts write, update, or delete different memories, they no longer share the same intermediate memory state, making trajectory-level comparisons fundamentally unfair. This violates a key assumption behind group-relative methods such as GRPO, where rollouts are compared as if they were sampled from the same effective environment. Consequently, trajectory-level rewards provide noisy or biased credit signals for long-horizon memory operations. To address this challenge, we introduce Memory-R2, a training framework for long-horizon memory-augmented LLM agents. Its core algorithm, LoGo-GRPO, combines local and global group-relative optimization. The global objective preserves end-to-end learning from long-horizon trajectory-level rewards, while local rerollouts compare different memory-operation outcomes from the same intermediate memory state, yielding fairer group comparisons and more precise supervision for memory construction. Beyond credit assignment, Memory-R2 jointly optimizes memory formation and memory evolution with a shared-parameter co-learning design, where a fact extractor and a memory manager are instantiated from the same LLM backbone through role-specific prompts. To stabilize multi-step RL over long memory horizons, we adopt a progressive curriculum that increases the training horizon from 8 to 16 to 32 sessions. Together, these components provide an effective training paradigm for memory-augmented LLM agents in long-horizon multi-session settings.

</details>


### [85] [Provable Robustness against Backdoor Attacks via the Primal-Dual Perspective on Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21780)
*Aman Saxena,Jan Schuchardt,Yan Scholten,Stephan Günnemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过连接随机平滑与差分隐私的隐私轮廓（privacy profiles），提出了一种模块化、紧致的端到端鲁棒性认证框架，能够统一处理训练时（后门投毒）和推理时（规避攻击）的联合扰动，并在DP-SGD和深度分区聚合上实例化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机平滑的鲁棒性认证主要针对训练时中毒攻击或推理时规避攻击单独处理，而现实中后门攻击同时扰动训练数据和测试数据，需要在一个统一的证书中分析训练时和测试时的随机化机制，这极具挑战性。

Method: 将随机平滑与差分隐私的隐私轮廓（privacy profiles）相关联，通过隐私轮廓为异构机制的组合提供数值化组合过程，从而实现对复杂组合机制的紧致、模块化、端到端认证，并利用已有的差分隐私机制分析结果。

Result: 在DP-SGD和深度分区聚合（Deep Partition Aggregation）上实例化框架，联合推导了针对训练时和推理时攻击的联合鲁棒性保证；在MNIST和CIFAR-10上实验验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 提供了一个原则性的通用框架，利用组合机制在更贴近真实威胁模型（同时存在训练时和推理时攻击）的场景下认证鲁棒性。

Abstract: Randomized smoothing is a powerful tool for certifying robustness to adversarial perturbations, including poisoning attacks via randomized training and evasion attacks via randomized inference. Extending these guarantees to backdoor attacks, where training and test data are jointly perturbed, remains challenging because training- and test-time randomized mechanisms must be analyzed within a single robustness certificate. We address this by connecting randomized smoothing to the dual view of differential privacy through privacy profiles, which provide a numerical procedure for composing heterogeneous mechanisms. The resulting framework enables tight, modular, end-to-end certification of complex, composed mechanisms while leveraging existing analyses of differentially private mechanisms. We instantiate the framework for DP-SGD and Deep Partition Aggregation with inference-time smoothing, deriving joint robustness guarantees against both training-time and inference-time attacks. Experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework. Overall, we provide a principled and general framework for using composite mechanisms to certify robustness under complex threat models that better capture the capabilities of real-world adversaries.

</details>


### [86] [MMD-Balls as Credal Sets: A PAC-Bayesian Framework for Epistemic Uncertainty in Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21783)
*Ahanaf Hasan Ariq*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个PAC-Bayesian理论框架，通过MMD（最大均值差异）参数化源域与目标域分布漂移的泛化界，并将MMD球解释为Walley不精确概率理论中的信度集，从而实现对认知不确定性（epistemic uncertainty）的形式化量化。论文建立了四个核心理论结果，并证明核引导的自适应可保护局部特征几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时自适应（TTA）方法虽能提升分布漂移下的模型性能，但缺乏形式化保证来连接漂移程度与预测可靠性。作者希望填补这一理论空白，提供可量化预测不确定性的严格框架。

Method: (i) 在RKHS-Lipschitz损失假设下，推导出含MMD依赖漂移惩罚项的PAC-Bayesian界；(ii) 通过MMD浓度不等式给出有限样本版本；(iii) 在信度集上建立均匀最坏情况风险界，并分解为下界风险与上界风险；(iv) 推导测地线保持界，证明核引导自适应可保护局部特征几何。

Result: 获得了四个关键理论结果：MMD参数化的泛化界、有限样本浓度版本、最坏情况风险下上界分解、以及特征几何保持界。信度集解释成功分离了认知不确定性与偶然不确定性（aleatoric uncertainty）。

Conclusion: 该方法为TTA何时应该执行提供了原则性的决策准则，即当认知不确定性过高时不应进行自适应，从而提升了预测可靠性。这一理论框架为分布漂移下的模型评估提供了形式化保障。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) methods improve model performance under distribution shift but lack formal guarantees connecting shift magnitude to prediction reliability. We develop a PAC-Bayesian framework yielding generalization bounds explicitly parameterized by the maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) between source and target distributions. Our principal contribution is interpreting MMD-balls around the source distribution as credal sets in Walley's imprecise probability theory, yielding natural epistemic uncertainty quantification. We establish: (i) a PAC-Bayesian bound with an MMD-dependent shift penalty under an RKHS-Lipschitz loss assumption; (ii) a finite-sample version via MMD concentration; (iii) a uniform worst-case risk bound over all distributions in the credal set, with a lower-upper risk decomposition; and (iv) geodesic preservation bounds explaining why kernel-guided adaptation protects local feature geometry. The credal set interpretation separates epistemic from aleatoric uncertainty and provides a principled decision criterion for when adaptation is warranted.

</details>


### [87] [stable-worldmodel: A Platform for Reproducible World Modeling Research and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21800)
*Lucas Maes,Quentin Le Lidec,Luiz Facury,Nassim Massaudi,Ayush Chaurasia,Francesco Capuano,Richard Gao,Taj Gillin,Dan Haramati,Damien Scieur,Yann LeCun,Randall Balestriero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了stable-worldmodel (swm)，一个开源的标准化世界模型研究平台，通过高性能数据层、标准化基线实现和系统化评估套件，解决了世界模型研究中代码库碎片化、数据加载慢和缺乏标准化泛化基准三大瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前世界模型研究存在严重的碎片化问题——不同的代码库、数据处理流程和评估协议阻碍了可复现性和公平比较。此外，三个关键瓶颈限制了当前实践：脆弱的单次性代码库、缓慢的视频数据加载以及缺乏标准化的泛化基准。

Method: 构建了stable-worldmodel (swm)平台，包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于Lance的高性能数据层，原生支持MP4、HDF5和LeRobot数据集并提供转换工具；(2) 现代世界模型基线和规划求解器的干净、经过充分测试的实现；(3) 扩展了可控视觉、几何和物理变化因素的环境与任务套件，用于系统性地评估动态理解、控制性能、表示质量和分布外泛化。

Result: swm平台将完整的world model pipeline统一在单一可扩展框架下，显著降低了研究开销，加速了向可靠世界模型的可信进展。

Conclusion: 通过统一标准化框架，swm有望推动世界模型研究的可复现性和公平比较，促进该领域更可靠的发展。

Abstract: World models are central to building agents that can reason, plan, and generalize beyond their training data. However, research on world models is currently fragmented, with disparate codebases, data pipelines, and evaluation protocols hindering reproducibility and fair comparison. Current practice is further limited by three key bottlenecks: fragile one-off codebases, slow video data loading, and the lack of standardized generalization benchmarks. We present stable-worldmodel (swm), an open-source platform for standardized and reproducible world modeling research and evaluation. It delivers (1) a high-performance Lance-based data layer with native support and conversion tools for MP4, HDF5, and LeRobot datasets, (2) clean, well-tested implementations of modern world model baselines and planning solvers, and (3) a broad suite of environments and tasks extended with controllable visual, geometric, and physical factors of variation for systematic in-silico evaluation of dynamics understanding, control performance, representation quality, and out-of-distribution generalization. By unifying the full pipeline under a single, scalable framework, \texttt{swm} dramatically reduces research overhead and accelerates trustworthy progress toward reliable world models.

</details>


### [88] [Why Semantic Entropy Fails: Geometry-Aware and Calibrated Uncertainty for Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21801)
*Zheyuan Zhang,Kaiwen Shi,Han Bao,Zehong Wang,Tianyi Ma,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出几何感知校准策略优化（GCPO）框架，通过将不确定性信号与梯度方差和学习信号质量建立理论联系，解决了现有熵基估计器存在的各向异性间隙和校准间隙问题，在多个基准上持续提升后训练性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前无批评者模型虽能利用模型生成输出进行可扩展学习，但缺乏区分信息性信号与噪声信号的原则性机制。现有基于响应级不确定性信号（如熵）来调控GRPO等组优化方法虽取得一定经验成功，但其对优化动态的影响机制不清晰且不稳定。

Method: (1) 首次从原理上将不确定性信号阐释为表征和调控梯度方差及学习信号质量的机制；(2) 识别了当前熵基估计器的两个关键缺陷：各向异性间隙和校准间隙；(3) 提出GCPO框架，集成几何感知度量以捕获语义分歧，并引入基于奖励的校准使不确定性信号与学习信号强度对齐。

Result: 在多个基准实验上，GCPO更忠实地跟踪梯度变异性，并持续提升后训练性能。

Conclusion: 设计与优化动态对齐的不确定性信号对于鲁棒的后训练至关重要，本研究为这一方向提供了原则性视角。

Abstract: Post-training has become central to improving reasoning and alignment in large language models, where critic-free models enable scalable learning from model-generated outputs but lack principled mechanisms to distinguish informative from noisy signals. Recent approaches leverage response-level measures as uncertainty signals to regulate group-based optimization methods such as GRPO. Yet their empirical success remains unstable and unclear in how they influence optimization dynamics. In this paper, we provide, to our knowledge, the first principled formulation that interprets uncertainty signals as mechanisms for characterizing and regulating gradient variance and learning signal quality. Based on both empirical and theoretical analysis, we identify two critical gaps of current entropy-based estimators: The anisotropic gap and The calibration gap. Motivated by this analysis, we propose Geometric-aware Calibrated Policy Optimization (GCPO), a novel framework integrating geometry-aware measures to capture semantic disagreement with reward-based calibration to align uncertainty with learning signal strength. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that our approach more faithfully tracks gradient variability and consistently improves post-training performance. Our results highlight the importance of designing uncertainty signals that are aligned with optimization dynamics, offering a principled perspective for robust post-training.

</details>


### [89] [Same Architecture, Different Capacity: Optimizer-Induced Spectral Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21803)
*Nandan Kumar Jha,Brandon Reagen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现优化器是表示扩展的关键轴：不同优化器（AdamW vs Muon）在相同架构下导致完全不同的FFN表示谱缩放定律，且优化器对谱结构的影响常超过架构干预。即使困惑度匹配，表示结构也可能截然不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缩放定律将优化器视为固定训练细节，忽略了优化器如何影响表示谱空间的利用效率，本文旨在揭示优化器作为表示扩展的独立轴的重要性。

Method: 通过FFN表示的特征谱（软/硬谱秩）测量不同优化器下的谱缩放定律；比较AdamW与Muon在稀有Token（TAIL）表示上的谱缩放指数；通过低秩变体Dion和延长训练控制困惑度匹配，分离损失与表示结构；对比优化器效应与架构干预（注意力秩、位置编码）的效应大小。

Result: Muon在TAIL表示上实现线性硬谱缩放（β=1.02），而AdamW仅为弱缩放（β=0.44），缩放指数提升2.3倍；困惑度匹配时谱几何仍显著不同；优化器引起的谱变化常超过架构干预。

Conclusion: 优化器应被视为表示扩展的第一类轴，优化器与架构需协同设计。

Abstract: Scaling laws have made language-model performance predictable from model size, data, and compute, but they typically treat the optimizer as a fixed training detail. We show that this assumption misses a fundamental axis of representation scaling: how effectively the optimizer converts added FFN width into utilized spectral capacity. Using eigenspectra of feed-forward network representations, measured through soft and hard spectral-ranks, we find that \emph{the same Transformer architecture realizes markedly different spectral scaling laws when trained with different optimizers}. Holding architecture and width schedule fixed, AdamW exhibits weak hard-rank scaling ($β$=0.44) on rare-token (TAIL) representations where learning is known to be hardest, whereas Muon achieves linear scaling ($β$=1.02) in the same regimes, a $2.3\times$ increase in the scaling exponent. This difference is not reducible to validation loss: AdamW configurations can match low-rank Dion variants in perplexity, under extended training, while exhibiting sharply different spectral geometry, demonstrating that matched loss does not imply matched representation structure. Hard--soft rank asymmetry further reveals that optimizers differ not only in how much capacity is realized, but also in how that capacity is structured across eigenmodes. To disentangle optimizer effects from architectural ones, we compare against architectural interventions (e.g., attention rank and positional encoding), and find that optimizer-induced spectral shifts often exceed the architectural effects. These results suggest optimization as a first-class axis of representation scaling, motivating optimizer--architecture co-design.

</details>


### [90] [Symbolic Density Estimation for Discrete Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21813)
*Ziwen Liu,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出符号密度估计（SDE）框架，能够从数据中自动恢复闭式概率质量函数，通过组合基本解析操作在结构化搜索空间中进行无监督学习。该方法整合了领域特定的结构先验、进化搜索和有效性感知推断阶段，并支持零膨胀和有限混合等更丰富的分布族。实验表明，算法能准确恢复所有基准分布族并给出精确参数估计，在真实数据应用中发现的混合模型相比标准模型具有更好的拟合优度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散概率分布的可解释分布目录长期以来仅通过逐案数学推导逐步扩展，缺乏自动发现新分布的系统性方法。

Method: 提出符号密度估计（SDE）无监督框架，通过组合基本解析操作在结构化搜索空间中自动发现闭式概率质量函数，集成领域结构先验、进化搜索和有效性感知推断，并扩展至零膨胀和有限混合模型。

Result: 算法准确恢复所有基准分布族并给出精确参数估计；在真实数据中发现的混合模型相比标准模型具有更优拟合优度。

Conclusion: SDE框架能够自动发现可解释的离散概率分布，是传统逐案数学推导的有效补充，为统计建模中分布发现提供了新的自动化途径。

Abstract: Discrete probability laws underpin statistical modeling, yet the catalog of interpretable distributions has expanded only gradually through centuries of case-by-case mathematical derivations. We introduce symbolic density estimation (SDE), an unsupervised framework that automatically recovers closed-form probability mass functions by composing elementary analytic operations within a structured search space. Our method integrates domain-specific structural priors with evolutionary search and a validity-aware inference stage, and it extends to richer distribution families such as zero inflation and finite mixtures. To support systematic evaluation and future research, we contribute a benchmark dataset spanning a broad collection of commonly used discrete distributions. The proposed algorithm recovers all benchmark families with accurate parameter estimates. A real data application shows that it identifies concise and interpretable mixture models that improve goodness-of-fit over standard models.

</details>


### [91] [Beyond Scalar Objectives: Expert-Feedback-Driven Autonomous Experimentation for Scientific Discovery at the Nanoscale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21820)
*Ralph Bulanadi,Jefferey Baxter,Arpan Biswas,Hiroshi Funakubo,Dennis Meier,Jan Schultheiß,Rama Vasudevan,Yongtao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出深度核成对学习（DKPL）方法，通过整合人类专家判断替代传统标量目标函数，实现自主显微实验中的专家引导主动学习，在学习物理有意义纳米结构方面表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于贝叶斯优化的自主实验框架依赖预定义标量描述符引导实验，但在许多情况下难以确定合适的标量描述符，且可能无法捕捉跨学科专家才能识别的细微但重要的科学现象。

Method: 开发深度核成对学习（DKPL），通过让专家直接使用跨学科知识评估实验输出的相对优劣，从中学习潜在效用函数以指导后续自主显微实验，替代传统标量目标函数驱动的主动学习。

Result: 在已知标签的实验模型数据上，DKPL能有效学习物理有意义的纳米结构并优先选择高信息测量区域。在铁电畴壁分析中，DKPL能区分铋铁氧体中高/低特征畴壁角，并发现铒锰氧化物中头对头和尾对尾的畴壁特征。

Conclusion: DKPL建立了将专家知识整合进自主显微实验的范式，展示了超越标量指标驱动学习限制、解决更复杂科学问题的专家引导自驱动实验室新路径。

Abstract: Self-driving laboratories or autonomous experimentation are emerging as transformative platforms for accelerating scientific discovery. Bayesian optimization (BO) is among the most widely used machine learning frameworks for these purposes, but these BO-based frameworks rely on predefined scalar descriptors to guide experimentation. In many situations, the determination of an appropriate scalar descriptor can be challenging, and may fail to capture subtle yet scientifically important phenomena apparent to experts with interdisciplinary insight. To overcome this limitation, here we develop deep-kernel pairwise learning (DKPL), an approach for autonomous microscopy experiments which incorporates human expertise and interdisciplinary scientific knowledge into an active learning loop. Instead of relying on explicit scalar objectives, DKPL enables experts to directly evaluate which experimental output is more promising using interdisciplinary knowledge. DKPL then learns a latent utility function from these expert judgements to guide subsequent autonomous microscopy experiments. We demonstrate DKPL's performance in learning physically meaningful nanoscale structures while effectively prioritizing high-information measurement regions using an experimental model dataset with known ground truth. We further apply DKPL to analyze the character of ferroelectric domain walls, where we find DKPL capable of distinguishing between high and low characteristic domain-wall angles in bismuth ferrite, and able to discover both head-to-head and tail-to-tail domain-wall character in erbium manganite. This development establishes an approach to integrate expert knowledge into autonomous microscopy experiments and demonstrates a pathway toward expert-guided self-driving laboratories capable of addressing scientific problems beyond the limits of scalar-metrics-driven learning.

</details>


### [92] [On-Policy Consistency Training Improves LLM Safety with Minimal Capability Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21834)
*Andy Han,Kristina Fujimoto,Avidan Shah,Kiet Nguyen,Kai Xu,Chen Yueh-Han,Ilia Sucholutsky,Rico Angell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的对齐方法——在线策略一致性训练（OPCT），通过在模型自身的响应上计算一致性目标，显著优于传统的离线监督微调（SFT）方式。在谄媚、越狱攻击和安全意识三个安全维度上，OPCT均表现出更强的泛化能力和更少的性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的一致性训练方法使用离线生成的监督信号进行SFT，导致模型仅记忆训练分布的表面形式，泛化能力差且能力退化严重。为克服这些问题，作者提出在线策略一致性训练，使模型在自己的输出上学习一致性约束。

Method: OPCT的核心思想是：对于每个提示，模型先生成自己的响应，然后利用对应的对比提示（contrastive prompts）作为条件，让模型对自身响应进行自监督优化，从而在线地计算一致性训练目标，而非使用预先生成的标签。

Result: OPCT在三个模型家族上均优于SFT基线：谄媚率从15.4%降至8.1%（SFT为11.2%）；在自适应逐目标攻击下，越狱防御成功率接近99%（SFT为87%）；安全意识在两个模型上超越SFT，一个模型持平；同时避免了SFT导致的MATH-500上28个百分点的性能下降。

Conclusion: 一致性训练最好以OPCT而非SFT的方式实现，尤其是在需要超越训练分布进行泛化的场景下。OPCT在提升安全性的同时显著减少了模型能力的退化。

Abstract: Aligned models can misbehave in several ways: they are often sycophantic, fall victim to jailbreaks, or fail to include appropriate safety warnings. Consistency training is a promising new alignment paradigm to mitigate such failures by training invariants into the model using contrastive input pairs. Existing consistency training procedures generate the supervision signal once, offline, and use supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to update the model. Unfortunately, the resulting models tend to merely memorize the surface forms of the training distribution and thus generalize poorly and regress in their capabilities. We introduce On-Policy Consistency Training (OPCT), a new consistency training approach where the objective is computed over the model's own responses to prompts, supervised by itself conditioned on corresponding contrastive prompts. We evaluate OPCT on three safety axes: sycophancy, jailbreaking, and safety awareness. Across three model families, OPCT outperforms its SFT counterpart on all safety desiderata. It nearly halves the sycophancy rate relative to baseline (8.1% vs. 15.4%, compared to 11.2% for SFT). Under an adaptive per-target attacker, OPCT holds jailbreak defense success near 99% on held-out jailbreak behaviors, whereas SFT achieves 87% on average. On safety awareness, OPCT outperforms SFT in two out of three models, and matches it on the other. OPCT also largely avoids the capability regressions that SFT induces, such as a 28-point drop on MATH-500. Our results suggest that consistency training is best implemented as OPCT rather than as SFT, especially when generalization beyond the training distribution is desired.

</details>


### [93] [Energy-Gated Attention: Spectral Salience as an Inductive Bias for Transformer Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21842)
*Athanasios Zeris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出能量门控注意力（EGA），通过计算token嵌入的谱能量来修改标准Transformer注意力，使信息密集的token获得更多注意力。在TinyShakespeare和Penn Treebank上分别取得+0.103和+0.101的验证损失改善，仅增加12,480个参数（<0.26%开销），并发现最优能量方向是数据自适应的，学习的能量阈值收敛于τ≈0.35，对应英文文本中携带高于平均谱能量的token比例（约36%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer注意力将所有token视为同等显著，忽略了token内在的信息内容差异。受湍流动力学中相干结构（携带不成比例能量的空间组织模式）的启发，作者提出信息密集位置（形态边界、句法中心、话语标记）在注意力中应获得更多权重，而非均匀处理所有token。

Method: 提出能量门控注意力（EGA），通过一个学习到的线性投影计算key token嵌入的谱能量（发现嵌入场的主导谱模式），然后根据谱能量门控值聚合过程。仅增加一个线性层（12,480个参数），几乎不增加计算成本。

Result: TinyShakespeare上验证损失改善+0.103，Penn Treebank上+0.101，证明数据集无关性。消融实验显示固定小波基（Morlet、Daubechies db2/db4）次优，最优能量方向是数据自适应的。学习到的能量阈值收敛于τ≈0.35，对应约36%的token携带高于平均谱能量。

Conclusion: EGA以极小参数和计算开销显著提升Transformer性能，揭示了注意力机制中信息密度与谱能量的关联。固定结构化基（如小波）不适合该任务，数据自适应方法更优，同时指出学习小波包是未来值得探索的方向。

Abstract: Standard transformer attention computes pairwise similarity between queries and keys, treating all tokens as equally salient regardless of their intrinsic informational content. In turbulent fluid dynamics, coherent structures -- the energetically dominant, spatially organized patterns that persist amid background chaos -- carry a disproportionate fraction of total energy and govern all transport. We propose that tokens play an analogous role in transformer attention: informationally dense positions (morphological boundaries, syntactic heads, discourse markers) concentrate spectral energy and should attract proportionally more attention than background tokens (function words, repeated patterns, low-information filler). We propose Energy-Gated Attention (EGA): a simple modification that gates value aggregation by the spectral energy of key token embeddings, computed by a single learned linear projection that discovers the dominant spectral mode of the embedding field. On TinyShakespeare, EGA achieves +0.103 validation loss improvement with only 12,480 additional parameters (<0.26% overhead) and no measurable computational cost. The result is consistent on Penn Treebank (+0.101), demonstrating dataset independence. A systematic ablation across three wavelet families (fixed Morlet, Daubechies db2/db4, and a parametric Morlet) establishes that fixed structured bases are suboptimal -- the optimal energy direction is data-adaptive and non-sinusoidal -- while identifying learned wavelet packets as a promising open direction. The learned energy threshold converges to tau ~= 0.35 independently of initialization, corresponding to the fraction (~36%) of tokens carrying above-average spectral energy in English text, a stable linguistic property consistent with the fraction of content words in running English text.

</details>


### [94] [Geometry-Adaptive Explainer for Faithful Dictionary-Based Interpretability under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21849)
*Sungjun Lim,Heedong Kim,Andrew Lee,Kyungwoo Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分布偏移会旋转模型实际使用的子空间，导致字典解释器（如稀疏自编码器）失准。作者形式化了这种失准为"忠实性差距"，并提出几何自适应解释器（GAE），仅需无标注OOD激活即可通过几何对齐恢复字典与OOD活跃子空间的一致性，无需梯度更新，理论上界定了误差的二次上界，实验上在多个模型和OOD设定下匹配或超越了所有基于训练的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于字典的解释器（如稀疏自编码器、transcoder）在分布外（OOD）场景下的忠实性缺乏系统性的研究，而分布偏移会导致解释器字典与模型实际使用的子空间发生错位。

Method: 形式化定义了"忠实性差距"这一几何距离指标，用于衡量ID字典与OOD活跃子空间之间的错位程度；提出GAE方法，通过几何旋转对字典进行重新对齐，仅需无标签OOD激活数据，无需梯度更新。

Result: 理论证明GAE的过余损失由二阶矩偏移的二次方上界控制；实验上在多个模型和多种OOD环境下，GAE在因果忠实性上匹配甚至超越所有基于训练的基线方法。

Conclusion: GAE能有效弥合分布偏移下的忠实性差距，为机械可解释性在分布外场景的应用提供了轻量且高效的适配方案。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability aims to explain a model's behavior by identifying causally responsible internal structures. Dictionary-based explainers such as sparse autoencoders and transcoders are a primary tool, but their faithfulness under out-of-distribution (OOD) shift has received little systematic attention. We show that distribution shift rotates the subspace that the model actively uses, misaligning the explainer's dictionary trained on in-distribution (ID) activations. We formalize this misalignment as the faithfulness gap, a geometric distance between the ID dictionary and the OOD-active subspace, and show that it controls OOD faithfulness degradation. To reduce this gap, we propose the Geometry-Adaptive Explainer (GAE), which realigns the explainer's dictionary with the OOD-active subspace while preserving the original feature structure. This requires only unlabeled OOD activations and no gradient updates. We prove that GAE improves over the unadapted ID explainer, with excess loss bounded quadratically by the second-moment shift. Empirically, GAE even matches or surpasses all training-based baselines in causal faithfulness across multiple models and OOD settings.

</details>


### [95] [The Illusion of Reasoning: Exposing Evasive Data Contamination in LLMs via Zero-CoT Truncation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21856)
*Yifan Lan,Yuanpu Cao,Hanyu Wang,Lu Lin,Jinghui Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Zero-CoT Probe (ZCP)的新型黑盒检测方法，通过截断Chain-of-Thought推理过程并结合同构扰动参考数据集，有效检测大型语言模型中的显式和规避式数据污染，并提出了Contamination Confidence指标来量化污染的严重程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据污染(特别是通过改写基准数据等规避策略)严重破坏了LLM推理能力的客观评估，现有检测方法难以可靠检测此类隐蔽污染。

Method: ZCP方法利用"模型的推理步骤会主动掩盖其记忆"这一现象，通过截断整个CoT过程暴露潜在的捷径映射；同时将模型在原始基准上的零样本CoT性能与同构扰动参考数据集进行对比，以隔离记忆与模型内在问题解决能力；并引入Contamination Confidence指标量化污染可能性和严重程度。

Result: 在先前识别的污染模型和专门微调的污染模型上的大量实验表明，ZCP能够稳健检测直接和规避式数据污染。

Conclusion: ZCP通过暴露潜在记忆映射而非依赖推理过程本身，有效解决了现有方法难以检测规避式数据污染的局限，为LLM基准测试的公平评估提供了新的工具。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated impressive reasoning abilities across a wide range of tasks, but data contamination undermines the objective evaluation of these capabilities. This problem is further exacerbated by malicious model publishers who use evasive, or indirect, contamination strategies, such as paraphrasing benchmark data to evade existing detection methods and artificially boost leaderboard performance. Current approaches struggle to reliably detect such stealthy contamination. In this work, we uncover a critical phenomenon: a model's generated reasoning steps actively mask its underlying memorization. Inspired by this, we propose the Zero-CoT Probe (ZCP), a novel black-box detection method that deliberately truncates the entire Chain-of-Thought (CoT) process to expose latent shortcut mappings. To further isolate memorization from the model's intrinsic problem-solving capabilities, ZCP compares the model's zero-CoT performance on the original benchmark against an isomorphically perturbed reference dataset. Furthermore, we introduce Contamination Confidence, a metric that quantifies both the likelihood and severity of contamination, moving beyond simple binary classifications. Extensive experiments on both previously identified contaminated models and specially fine-tuned contaminated models demonstrate that ZCP robustly detects both direct and evasive data contamination. The code for ZCP is accessible at https://github.com/Yifan-Lan/zero-cot-probe.

</details>


### [96] [When to Switch, Not Just What: Transition Quality Prediction in Clash Royale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21868)
*Heeyun Heo,Huy Kang Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过对34,619名《部落冲突：皇室战争》玩家的92.6万场对局分析，发现频繁切换策略反而与低胜率相关。作者指出这源于推荐系统的"零切换成本假设"，并提出了三阶段决策框架TQP（Who→When→What），通过PersonaGate、TimingGate和ScoreFusion模块实现个性化策略推荐，同时设计了不依赖玩家选择作为最优基准的评估指标SwitchGap，最终在5.4%推荐率下达到+10.4个百分点的SwitchGap提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统假设策略切换无成本，忽视了切换行为本身带来的胜率损失以及玩家个体在切换倾向上的差异，导致频繁切换者胜率更低。

Method: 提出TQP三阶段流水线：1) PersonaGate：抑制对"保持策略更优"类玩家的推荐；2) TimingGate：通过子类型和状态匹配的基线识别切换净收益时机；3) ScoreFusion：结合采纳信号与预测转移质量（Δ胜率）对候选策略排序。并引入SwitchGap评估指标，不将玩家实际选择视为最优。

Result: TQP在5.4%推荐率下实现SwitchGap提升+10.4个百分点；损失触发的切换者（胜率最低群体）从子类型条件引导中获益最大。

Conclusion: 考虑切换成本的个性化策略推荐能有效提升胜率，尤其对低胜率玩家群体效果显著，验证了"零切换成本假设"的局限性。

Abstract: In competitive games, players frequently switch strategies after losing streaks, yet our analysis of 926,334 match records from 34,619 Clash Royale players reveals a counterintuitive pattern: switching frequency is inversely associated with the win rate, with effects that vary substantially across players and situational contexts. We attribute this to a limitation common in many prior recommendation systems, which evaluate strategies by expected quality while overlooking the behavioral cost of switching and individual differences in switching propensity. We refer to this implicit premise as the Zero Switching Cost Assumption. To address this, we reformulate strategy recommendation as a transition-level decision problem and instantiate it as TQP (Transition Quality Predictor), a three-stage pipeline structured as Who -> When -> What. PersonaGate suppresses recommendations for players whose strategic consistency is empirically associated with superior outcomes. TimingGate identifies moments when switching is likely to yield a net benefit over staying, using a subtype- and state-matched baseline to control for natural win-rate recovery. ScoreFusion ranks candidate strategies by combining an adoptability signal with predicted transition quality (delta WR). We further introduce SwitchGap, an evaluation metric that measures a policy's discriminative quality without treating observed player choices as optimal ground truth. This property is particularly important because the most frequent switchers record the lowest win rates. The full pipeline achieves a SwitchGap of +10.4 percentage points at a recommendation rate of 5.4%, and loss-triggered switchers, despite being the lowest-performing group, benefit the most from subtype-conditioned guidance.

</details>


### [97] [Noise Schedule Design for Diffusion Models: An Optimal Control Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21911)
*Seo Taek Kong,Weina Wang,R. Srikant*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于最优控制理论的扩散模型噪声调度设计框架，将噪声调度问题转化为Fisher信息量演化的最优控制问题，证明了该框架可实现$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(d/n)$的采样误差界，并在参数化假设下得到可调参数的闭式噪声调度表达式，在图像生成任务上取得更优的FID分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型的理论工作证明了$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(d/n)$采样误差界的可达性，但仅适用于特定噪声调度，不包括实践中常用的调度方案。本文旨在建立统一的噪声调度分析与设计框架，弥合理论与实践之间的差距。

Method: 将噪声调度设计问题重述为最优控制问题：状态变量为扩散过程的Fisher信息量（遵循ODE演化），控制输入为噪声调度；目标函数为Fisher信息量的泛函（被证明是KL散度采样误差的上界）；通过求解该最优控制问题获得噪声调度的充分条件；在数据分布参数化假设下推导闭式噪声调度表达式。

Result: 获得了噪声调度需满足的充分条件，确保$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(d/n)$的最优采样误差界；推导出泛化指数调度和Sigmoid调度的可调参数闭式表达式；系统调参后的新调度在图像生成基准上取得了更优的FID分数。

Conclusion: 该框架为扩散模型噪声调度提供了理论驱动的设计原则，既能恢复实践中使用的经典调度，又通过引入可调参数实现性能提升，有效连接了理论分析与实际应用。

Abstract: We develop a principled framework for analyzing and designing noise schedules in diffusion models. We show that one can recast this design problem as an optimal control problem, whose state is the Fisher information of the diffusion process which evolves according to an ODE and the control input is the noise schedule. The objective of the optimal control problem is a functional involving the Fisher information, which is shown to be an upper bound on the Kullback-Leibler sampling error. By solving this optimal control problem, we obtain sufficient conditions on noise schedules under which state-of-the-art $\tilde{\mathcal{O}} (d/n)$ sampling error is achievable, where $d$ is the data dimension and $n$ is the number of discretization steps. While existing theoretical work also prove that $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(d/n)$ sampling error bounds are achievable, these results hold for specific noise schedules, which do not include the schedules used in practice. Under a further parametric assumption on the data distribution, we show that one can obtain closed-form expressions for the noise schedules. These noise schedules generalize standard empirical schedules such as exponential and sigmoid schedules by allowing additional parameters that can be tuned. Systematically tuning the parameters of these schedules yields new schedules that achieve superior FID scores on image generation benchmarks.

</details>


### [98] [CausalGuard: Conformal Inference under Graph Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21928)
*Vikash Singh,Weicong Chen,Debargha Ganguly,Yanyan Zhang,Nengbo Wang,Sreehari Sankar,Mohsen Hariri,Alexander Nemecek,Chaoda Song,Shouren Wang,Biyao Zhang,Van Yang,Erman Ayday,Jing Ma,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CausalGuard 是一种结构加权共形框架，通过聚合图条件双重鲁棒伪结果，利用LLM先验和BIC重新加权候选DAG，实现分布自由的有限样本边际覆盖，在五个基准测试中达到90%以上名义覆盖率并减少区间宽度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从观测数据估计处理效应需要选择调整集，但有效调整依赖于未知的因果图。图误设定会导致覆盖不足，而图无关的共形方法只能通过大幅填充恢复名义覆盖。需要一种对图结构稳健的推断方法。

Method: 提出CausalGuard框架：(1) 从LLM导出的边先验生成候选DAG，用条件独立性检验剪枝，通过BIC重新加权；(2) 聚合图条件双重鲁棒伪结果；(3) 构建复合非一致性分数对后验加权伪结果进行校准。

Result: 在五个基准测试中，CausalGuard在可直接评估的目标上达到90%以上平均覆盖，且区间宽度小于需要大幅填充的图无关共形基线。压力测试表明其能抑制无效的碰撞器调整，并在先验误设时保持稳定。

Conclusion: CausalGuard提供了分布自由的有限样本边际覆盖保证；在因果识别、重叠、条件均值 nuisance 稳定性和目标对齐有效调整策略的集中条件下，其条件均值收敛到真实条件平均处理效应。

Abstract: Estimating treatment effects from observational data requires choosing an adjustment set, but valid adjustment depends on an unknown causal graph. Graph misspecification can cause under-coverage, while graph-agnostic conformal wrappers may regain nominal coverage only through large padding. We introduce CausalGuard, a structure-weighted conformal framework that calibrates after aggregating graph-conditional doubly robust pseudo-outcomes. Candidate DAGs are proposed from an LLM-derived edge prior, pruned by conditional-independence tests, and reweighted by Bayesian Information Criterion. A composite nonconformity score then calibrates the posterior-weighted pseudo-outcome. CausalGuard provides distribution-free finite-sample marginal coverage for this aggregated pseudo-outcome; under causal identification, overlap, conditional-mean nuisance stability, and concentration on target-aligned valid adjustment strategies, its conditional mean converges to the true Conditional Average Treatment Effect. Across five benchmarks, CausalGuard attains mean coverage above the nominal 90% level for the directly evaluable target and reduces width when graph-agnostic conformal baselines require large padding. Stress tests show that CausalGuard suppresses invalid collider adjustment and remains stable under misspecified priors when the retained candidate set is data-supported.

</details>


### [99] [Optimal Guarantees for Auditing Rényi Differentially Private Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21938)
*Benjamin D. Kim,Lav R. Varshney,Daniel Alabi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于假设检验的黑盒审计框架，利用Donsker-Varadhan变分估计器直接估计Rényi散度，以审计声称满足Rényi差分隐私（RDP）的机器学习算法。该框架提供了显式且非渐近的置信区间，并证明了匹配的极小化最优下界，表明其样本复杂度在信息论意义上是最优的。在DP-SGD的完全黑盒审计实验中，该方法在MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上显著优于现有方法，尤其在小到中等Rényi阶数下表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒审计方法在估计Rényi差分隐私（RDP）的下界时，缺乏严格的统计保证和最优的样本复杂度，尤其是在小到中等Rényi阶数下审计精度不足。本文旨在通过变分估计器提供理论上最优且具有严格置信区间的审计方法。

Method: 基于假设检验框架，使用Donsker-Varadhan（DV）变分估计器直接估计相邻执行之间的Rényi散度；通过类别受限的DV估计器推导显式的非渐近置信区间，将统计估计误差与算法隐私泄露分离；并证明极小化匹配下界以确立信息论最优性。

Result: 在DP-SGD的完全黑盒审计中，该方法在MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上，在广泛的隐私机制范围内，相比现有最优黑盒方法实现了整体的大幅改进，尤其在小到中等Rényi阶数下效果显著。

Conclusion: 该框架首次为RDP的DV估计审计建立了最优的理论保证，并通过实证验证了其有效性，为黑盒差分隐私审计提供了严格且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: We study black-box auditing for machine learning algorithms that claim R \ 'enyi differential privacy (RDP) guarantees. We introduce an auditing framework, based on hypothesis testing, that directly estimates Rényi divergence between neighboring executions using the Donsker-Varadhan (DV) variational estimator. Our analysis yields explicit and non-asymptotic confidence intervals for RDP auditing via class-restricted DV estimators, separating statistical estimation error from algorithmic privacy leakage. We prove matching minimax lower bounds showing that, up to logarithmic factors, our sample-complexity guarantees are information-theoretically optimal, thereby establishing the first optimal guarantees for auditing RDP via DV estimators. Empirically, we instantiate our framework for auditing DP-SGD in a fully black-box setting. Across MNIST and CIFAR-10, and over a wide range of privacy regimes, our auditors produce a strong overall improvement on empirical RDP lower bounds compared to prior state-of-the-art black-box methods especially at small and moderate Rényi orders where accurate auditing is most challenging.

</details>


### [100] [SCI-Defense: Defending Manipulation Attacks from Generative Engine Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21948)
*Xucheng Yu,Haibo Jin,Huimin Zeng,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SCI-Defense防御框架，通过困惑度检测、语义完整性评分和候选间检测三组件对抗生成式引擎优化攻击。在亚马逊商品描述数据集上达到完美精确率与零假阳性率，但在MS MARCO网页段落上对特定攻击召回率极低，揭示了现有防御机制的盲点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的排序系统容易受到生成式引擎优化（GEO）攻击， adversaries通过向商品描述注入语义信号人为提升排名，现有防御手段（如PPL过滤、内容分类器、释义）对此类语义操纵攻击完全无效，亟需新的防御方案。

Method: 提出三组件防御框架SCI-Defense：1) 困惑度检测（PPL）；2) 语义完整性评分（SIS），评估四个操纵维度——权威归因（AA）、叙事目的性（NP）、比较性声明（CA）和时间性声明（TC）；3) 候选间检测（ICD）。

Result: 在600个亚马逊商品描述（6个类别）上，SCI-Defense对字符串攻击、推理攻击和评论攻击的召回率分别为1.000、0.952和0.830，精确率1.000，假阳性率0.000。在600个MS MARCO网页段落上，字符串攻击被完美阻断，但评论攻击召回率接近零。现有防御（PPL过滤器、SafetyClf、释义）对语义操纵攻击召回率为零。

Conclusion: SCI-Defense有效防御针对电商商品描述的GEO攻击，但揭示了语义相关性操纵（如规格放大、用例饱和攻击）是结构性防御盲点，为未来研究指明方向。

Abstract: LLM-based ranking systems are vulnerable to Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) attacks, where adversaries inject semantic signals into product descriptions to artificially boost rankings. We propose SCI-Defense, a three-component defense framework combining Perplexity detection (PPL), Semantic Integrity Scoring (SIS), and Inter-Candidate Detection (ICD). SIS evaluates four manipulation dimensions: Authority Attribution (AA), Narrative Purposiveness (NP), Comparative Claims (CA), and Temporal Claims (TC). Evaluated on 600 Amazon product descriptions across 6 categories, SCI-Defense achieves Precision=1.000 and FPR=0.000, with Recall of 1.000, 0.952, and 0.830 against String, Reasoning, and Review attacks respectively. On 600 MS MARCO web passages, String attacks are blocked with perfect recall while Review attacks yield near-zero recall, as web passages lack the persuasion-oriented signals that SIS targets in product descriptions. We demonstrate that existing defenses -- PPL-only filters, SafetyClf content classifiers, and paraphrasing -- achieve zero recall against semantic manipulation attacks. We further demonstrate new attacks such as Specification Amplification and Use-Case Saturation can expose semantic relevance manipulation as a structural defense blind spot that suggests directions for future research.

</details>


### [101] [Dynamic Mixture of Latent Memories for Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21951)
*Dianzhi Yu,Vireo Zhang,Hongru Wang,Yanyu Chen,Minda Hu,Wanghan Xu,Siki Chen,Philip Torr,Zhenfei Yin,Irwin King*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MoLEM框架，基于动态混合专家模型的生成式潜在记忆机制，通过冻结基模型、将经验知识内化到额外模块中，实现持续学习而不遗忘，在数学、科学和编程领域平均准确率提升10.40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体自我进化方法中，参数更新会导致灾难性遗忘，而外部记忆无法真正增强模型内在能力，亟需一种既能持续积累新知识又不遗忘已有能力的框架。

Method: MoLEM框架将多个专家视为独立的记忆载体，通过路由器的键-值匹配选择和加权专家，将聚合的潜在记忆注入推理过程；基模型完全冻结，所有经验知识内化到额外模块；每个训练阶段配有一个轻量级自编码器，在推理时选择合适路由组，无匹配输入则回退到预训练模型。

Result: 在数学、科学和编程领域的持续学习序列上，完整序列后平均准确率比原始预训练基线提升10.40%，且所有对比方法均无法在不同训练顺序下持续超越该基线。

Conclusion: MoLEM通过冻结基模型、以动态MoE+潜在记忆方式实现持续学习，有效避免灾难性遗忘，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Achieving self-evolution in intelligent agents requires the continual accumulation of new knowledge across changing task sequences without forgetting previously acquired abilities. Existing approaches either internalize knowledge by updating model parameters, which induces catastrophic forgetting, or rely on external memory, which fails to genuinely enhance the model's intrinsic capabilities. We propose MoLEM, a generative mixture of latent memory framework based on a dynamic mixture-of-experts (MoE). We treat multiple experts as independent carriers to generate memory. A router selects and weights experts through key-query matching, and the aggregated latent memory is injected into the reasoning process. The base model for reasoning remains entirely frozen, with all experiential knowledge internalized into the additional modules, avoiding catastrophic forgetting. For continual learning, each training stage is paired with a lightweight autoencoder that selects the appropriate routing group at inference, and inputs that match no stage fall back to the pretrained model. Experiments train the framework on continual-learning sequences spanning math, science, and code domains. After training, we evaluate the framework on the corresponding test sets to measure task learning and competence preservation across continual adaptation stages. After the full continual-learning sequence, our method improves the average accuracy by 10.40% over the Vanilla pretrained baseline, while none of the competing methods consistently exceed this baseline across different training orders.

</details>


### [102] [ChronoMedicalWorld: A Medical World Model for Learning Patient Trajectories from Longitudinal Care Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21963)
*Jiangyuan Wang,Xuyong Chen,Junwei He,Xu Xu,Shasha Xie,Fuman Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为ChronoMedicalWorld Model (CMWM)的动作条件潜世界模型框架，用于从纵向护理数据中学习患者轨迹。通过在慢性肾病（CKD）的年度eGFR轨迹预测任务上进行验证，该模型在动态50%历史回滚测试中实现了MAE 7.384和RMSE 10.256，优于调优的GPT-5.5结构化提示基线（分别降低7.28%和7.35%），优势主要来自患者与健康教练之间的对话部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期临床模拟（预测患者在指定干预下数年内的生理演变）对慢性疾病护理至关重要，但现有的EHR模型主要是判别式的，而通用大语言模型在重复干预下会产生漂移。本文旨在解决这一挑战，提出一个能够有效学习患者轨迹并处理结构化干预和自由文本沟通的框架。

Method: CMWM采用动作条件潜世界模型框架，包含三个关键组件：（1）联合嵌入状态编码器；（2）宽动作编码器（可同时接受结构化干预指标和自由文本通信嵌入）；（3）循环潜变量过渡模块。模型在六项目标下训练：下一观测监督、下一潜变量预测、SIGReg潜变量正则化，以及三个生理学感知形状先验（斜率、连续性、大跳跃惩罚）。此外，采用闭环回滚前缀协议使训练与部署对齐。

Result: 在2,232名患者的肾病队列上，CKD实例化在动态50%历史回滚测试中实现MAE 7.384和RMSE 10.256，优于GPT-5.5结构化提示基线（MAE 7.964，RMSE 11.069），分别降低7.28%和7.35%的误差。性能提升主要由患者-健康教练对话部分驱动。

Conclusion: CMWM框架并非CKD特定：其架构、损失设计和训练协议适用于任何可以建模为周期性临床状态与结构化和对话干预交错的慢性疾病。该工作展示了在长期临床模拟中结合结构化数据和自由文本信息的潜力。

Abstract: Long-horizon clinical simulation -- predicting how a patient's physiology evolves over years under specified interventions -- is central to chronic-disease care, yet existing electronic health record (EHR) models are predominantly discriminative, and general-purpose large language models drift under repeated interventions. We propose the \textbf{ChronoMedicalWorld Model (CMWM)}, an action-conditioned latent world-model framework for learning patient trajectories from longitudinal care data. CMWM couples a joint-embedding state encoder with a wide action encoder that admits both structured intervention indicators and free-text communication embeddings, and trains a recurrent latent transition module under a six-term objective: next-observation supervision, next-latent prediction, SIGReg latent regularisation, and three physiology-aware shape priors (slope, continuity, large-jump penalty). A closed-loop rollout-prefix protocol matches training to deployment, so the model is optimised against the same multi-step error it exhibits at inference. As a concrete case study, we instantiate CMWM for annual estimated glomerular filtration rate (eGFR) trajectory forecasting in chronic kidney disease (CKD). On a 2{,}232-patient nephrology cohort, the CKD instantiation achieves a dynamic-50\% history rollout test mean absolute error (MAE) of 7.384 and root-mean-square error (RMSE) of 10.256, against 7.964 and 11.069 for a tuned GPT-5.5 structured-prompting baseline ($-7.28\%$ MAE, $-7.35\%$ RMSE), with the gain dominated by the dialogue portion of patient--health-coach communication. The framework is not CKD-specific: its architecture, loss design, and training protocol apply to any chronic condition that can be cast as periodic clinical state interleaved with structured and conversational interventions.

</details>


### [103] [An Improved Adaptive PID Optimizer with Enhanced Convergence and Stability for Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21968)
*Saurabh Saini,Kapil Ahuja,Thomas Wick,Saurav Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对AdaPID优化器存在的收敛性和稳定性问题，通过融合AMSGrad的非递增有效学习率和DiffGrad的梯度差分调制因子，提出了新型IAdaPID-ADG优化器，在多个数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动量SGD存在梯度噪声大和过冲现象两大缺陷；Adam解决了噪声问题但存在收敛和稳定性问题；PID优化器解决了过冲问题；现有AdaPID虽结合了两者优势，但仍继承了Adam的收敛性和稳定性缺陷。

Method: 将AMSGrad中非递增有效学习率的思想和DiffGrad中基于梯度差分的调制因子创新性地集成到AdaPID框架中，提出IAdaPID-ADG优化器。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR10基准数据集以及IARC、AnnoCerv真实数据集上，IAdaPID-ADG显著超越所有对比优化器；在MNIST上的消融实验验证了各新增组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 结合非递增学习率和梯度差分调制因子的改进策略能有效解决AdaPID的收敛与稳定性问题，提出的IAdaPID-ADG是一种更优的深度学习优化器。

Abstract: Optimization is essential in deep learning. The foundational method upon which most optimizers are built is momentum-based stochastic gradient descent. However, it suffers from two key drawbacks. First, it has noisy and varying gradients, and second, it has an overshoot phenomenon. To address noisy gradients, Adam was proposed, which remains the most widely used adaptive optimizer. To address the overshoot phenomenon, a control-theory-based PID optimizer was proposed. To tackle both the limitations within a single framework, several variants of Adaptive PID (AdaPID) have recently been proposed.
  Although AdaPID performs well, it still inherits two critical drawbacks from Adam, namely convergence and stability issues. In this work, we address both these limitations. To fix the convergence issue, we uniquely integrate the idea of using a non-increasing effective learning rate into AdaPID (originally proposed in AMSGrad, an extension of Adam). To fix the stability issue, we innovatively integrate a gradient difference based modulation factor into AdaPID (originally proposed in DiffGrad, another extension of Adam). Combining both these ideas in AdaPID, results in our novel IAdaPID-ADG optimizer.
  We evaluate our proposed optimizer on multiple datasets, including benchmark datasets (MNIST and CIFAR10) and real-world datasets (IARC and AnnoCerv). The IAdaPID-ADG substantially outperforms all competing optimizers. Additionally, we perform an ablation study on the MNIST dataset to demonstrate the contribution of each added component.

</details>


### [104] [How Sparsity Allocation Shapes Label-Free Post-Pruning Recoverability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21972)
*Qishi Zhan,Minxuan Hu,Liang He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在无标签修复场景下，剪枝稀疏性分配（ERK vs LAMP）对后修复精度的影响，发现分配方式显著影响修复效果，且最佳分配随架构、数据集难度和稀疏度变化；同时识别出一个BatchNorm校准失效但基于激活统计的修复仍能恢复精度的可恢复过渡区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无标签后修复方法的效果受上游剪枝分配策略影响，但该影响尚未被系统研究；且实际部署中可能缺少标签数据用于重训练，需要探索无标签修复条件下不同分配策略的表现。

Method: 在CIFAR-10/100和Imagenette上使用ResNet-18/34/50，在90%-95.5%稀疏度下，对比ERK和LAMP两种剪枝分配策略，采用相同的固定激活统计修复后端；并在ImageNet-100和DenseNet-121上进行额外验证。

Result: 分配策略的选择在相同全局稀疏度下能显著改变后修复精度；最佳分配随架构、数据集难度和稀疏度变化；发现存在一个修复敏感过渡区间，其中BatchNorm校准开始失效但激活统计修复仍能恢复非平凡精度；该可恢复区间的位置和宽度受数据规模和连接结构影响。

Conclusion: 剪枝分配和剪枝后修复应联合研究，因为分配决定了可用于无标签修复的激活信号量。

Abstract: Unstructured magnitude pruning at high sparsity can reduce neural network accuracy to near-random performance, while labeled retraining may be unavailable in practical deployment settings. Label-free post-pruning repair methods can partially recover collapsed sparse models, but their effectiveness depends on the sparse model left by the upstream pruning allocation. This paper studies how sparsity allocation shapes post-repair recoverability under a fixed activation-statistic repair backend. We compare ERK and LAMP allocations under the same label-free repair protocol across CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Imagenette with ResNet-18, ResNet-34, and ResNet-50 at sparsities from 90% to 95.5%. The results show that allocation choice can substantially change post-repair accuracy at the same global sparsity, and that the preferred allocation varies with architecture, dataset difficulty, and sparsity level. We identify a repair-sensitive transition regime in which BatchNorm recalibration begins to fail, while activation-statistic repair still recovers nontrivial accuracy. Additional validation on ImageNet-100 and DenseNet-121 shows that the location and width of this recoverable regime depend on data scale and connectivity structure. These findings suggest that pruning allocation and post-pruning repair should be studied jointly, since the allocation determines how much activation signal remains available for label-free recovery.

</details>


### [105] [Reasoning through Verifiable Forecast Actions: Consistency-Grounded RL for Financial LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21975)
*Jialin Chen,Aosong Feng,Harshit Verma,Siyi Gu,Haiwen Wang,Ali Maatouk,Yixuan He,Yifeng Gao,Leandros Tassiulas,Rex Ying*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StockR1是一个时序增强的大语言模型，通过可验证的预测动作（forecast action）统一了股票预测和金融推理。该模型先发出结构化的定性市场展望表示，再调用条件时序解码器生成未来分布轨迹，并通过强化学习优化，在金融问答和股票预测上显著优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么将时间序列抽象为文本，要么将预测与语言推理解耦，导致定性推理与定量结果之间存在根本性不匹配。

Method: 基于工具调用设计，模型先发出结构化的预测动作（可解释的定性市场展望），再调用以该动作为条件的时序解码器生成未来分布轨迹；使用强化学习优化，奖励联合反映答案有效性、预测准确性以及生成动作与观测时序动态之间的一致性，并通过样本级不确定性标量对奖励进行重加权。

Result: 在大规模10年基准上，StockR1在推理准确率上相比基线提升17.7%（4B模型）和25.9%（8B模型），一致优于时序基线模型和通用大语言模型。

Conclusion: 结构化预测动作能够在语言推理与时间预测之间建立强大的协同效应，使LLM能够通过可验证、可解释且数值化 grounded 的决策进行推理。

Abstract: Financial markets are characterized by extreme non-stationarity, low signal-to-noise ratios, and strong dependence on external information such as news, company fundamentals, and macroeconomic signals. Yet, existing approaches either abstract time-series into text or decouple forecasting from language-based reasoning, leading to a fundamental mismatch between qualitative reasoning and quantitative outcomes. To address this, we introduce StockR1, a time-series-enhanced LLM that unifies stock forecasting and financial reasoning through a verifiable forecast action. Based on a tool-call design, the model first emits a forecast action, which is a structured and interpretable representation of its qualitative market outlook. It then invokes a time-series decoder conditioned on this action to generate distributional future trajectories, leading to more informed question answering and financial reasoning. We optimize the full pipeline with reinforcement learning, where rewards jointly reflect answer validity, forecast accuracy, and consistency between generated actions and observed time-series dynamics. In addition, rewards are reweighted by a sample-level uncertainty scalar, encouraging the model to accommodate varying uncertainty in market dynamics. We evaluate StockR1 on financial question answering and stock forecasting over a large-scale 10-year benchmark. Our method consistently outperforms time-series baselines and general-purpose LLMs, improving reasoning accuracy by 17.7% (4B) and 25.9% (8B). These findings demonstrate that structuring the forecast actions establishes a powerful synergy between language reasoning and temporal prediction, enabling LLMs to reason through verifiable, interpretable, and numerically grounded decisions.

</details>


### [106] [Check Your LLM's Secret Dictionary! Five Lines of Code Reveal What Your LLM Learned (Including What It Shouldn't Have)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22005)
*Hisashi Miyashita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出对大型语言模型的lm_head权重矩阵进行奇异值分解（仅需5行PyTorch代码，无需模型推理），即可直接从权重中揭示可解释的语义子空间。左奇异向量识别出与特定方向对齐时最易被选中的词汇簇，反映训练数据组成与策展哲学。分析GPT-OSS-120B、Gemma-2-2B和Qwen2.5-1.5B发现：GPT呈现功能分化的层次结构；Gemma以19世纪前英语正字法主导，形成阶梯式聚类结构；Qwen涵盖多语言但包含不宜直接发表的子空间。基座-指令对比表明伦理问题源于预训练且无法通过对齐移除。提出了词汇簇得分（VCS）和加权投影得分（WPS），后者可零推理检测glitch token。呼吁将lm_head SVD分析作为发布前安全审计标准步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全审计通常依赖大量推理计算，缺乏直接从模型权重中高效检测语义子空间和潜在安全问题（如glitch token、有害词汇簇）的方法，且训练数据组成与模型行为之间的关系难以量化分析。

Method: 对transformer LLM的lm_head权重矩阵进行SVD分解，提取左奇异向量作为语义子空间；定义词汇簇得分（VCS）量化子空间一致性；定义加权投影得分（WPS）作为静态glitch token检测器。在GPT-OSS-120B、Gemma-2-2B、Qwen2.5-1.5B三个模型上对比基座与指令微调版本。

Result: 发现不同模型的奇异值谱和词汇簇结构存在系统性差异：GPT具有功能分化层次结构；Gemma被古代英语正字法主导形成阶梯聚类；Qwen多语言覆盖但含不宜内容。WPS成功检测出已知glitch token shokubutsu-hyakka-tsu（ID 137606）。伦理问题子空间在基座模型中存在且指令微调无法移除。
